Don't use for non-ANR crashes, dropped frames, general CPU profiling, or Perfetto system traces. - **anr-deadlock-detective**: Investigate Android ANRs (Application Not Responding) and main-thread deadlocks from traces, logs, and Perfetto/systrace data. Covers main-thread blocks, binder transactions, lock contention, Choreographer skips, and coroutine-induced ANRs. Use whenever the user mentions ANR, "not responding" dialogs, "the app hangs", "input dispatching timed out", `anr/traces.txt`, Crashlytics ANR reports, or asks why a screen freezes. Also use when the dev has a stack trace from `adb bugreport` and needs help reading it. ([Mithrandir21/game-deals-app](https://github.com/Mithrandir21/game-deals-app/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/anr-deadlock-detective))
- **anr-detective**: Expert Android ANR analyzer and fixer. Use this skill whenever: - A user shares an ANR trace, tombstone, logcat, or bug report (.zip) - A user says their app is freezing, hanging, or getting ANR dialogs - A user wants to scan their Android codebase for ANR risks - A user pastes a stack trace with "ANR", "Binder", "main thread", or "not responding" - A user asks about blocking operations, deadlocks, or Android performance - The agent is about to write or modify Android Java/Kotlin code touching an Activity, Service, BroadcastReceiver, ContentProvider, Application, or JobService — audit for ANR… ([MuhiminOsim/anr-detective](https://github.com/MuhiminOsim/anr-detective/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **anr-watchdog-triage**: Diagnose hangs and OS kills that never throw: Android ANRs, iOS watchdog terminations and hangs, and memory-pressure kills such as jetsam. Map each kill to its cause first — an ANR trace whose main thread is blocked on a lock, disk I/O or a binder call; a watchdog kill because launch or resume ran out of time; a memory kill driven by peak footprint, not necessarily a leak — then read the main-thread stack, move blocking work off the main thread, and judge memory from a footprint timeline, not one heap snapshot. Use when someone says "our ANR rate is over the Play threshold", "0x8badf00d", "ki… ([greenblacked/AI](https://github.com/greenblacked/AI/tree/HEAD/plugins/client-apps/skills/anr-watchdog-triage))
